      132 +                                                              
      133 +                return Result<ConcurrencyToken>.Failure(      
      134 +                    ResultStatus.TransportFailure,            
      135 +                    "The update was sent without a concurrency
          + token. This is a defect.");                                  
      136 +            }                                                 
      137 +                                                              
      138 +            var status = _statusInterpreter.Interpret(response
          +.StatusCode, isSingleEntityRead: false);                      
      139 +                                                              
      140 +            if (status == ResultStatus.ConcurrencyConflict)   
      141 +            {                                                 
      142 +                _logger.LogInformation(                       
      143 +                    "Update to {UserName} conflicted: the reco
          +rd changed since it was read.",                               
      144 +                    update.UserName.Value);                   
      145 +                                                              
      146 +                return Result<ConcurrencyToken>.Conflict(     
      147 +                    "This person changed since you loaded them
          +. Reload and try again.");                                    
      148 +            }                                                 
      149 +                                                              
      150 +            if (status != ResultStatus.Success)               
      151 +            {                                                 
      152 +                return Failure<ConcurrencyToken>(status, respo
          +nse, requestUri);                                             
      153 +            }                                                 
      154 +                                                              
      155 +            var token = await ReadUpdatedTokenAsync(update.Use
          +rName, response, cancellationToken)                           
      156 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      157 +                                                              
      158 +            if (token is null)                                
      159 +            {                                                 
      160 +                return Result<ConcurrencyToken>.Failure(      
      161 +                    ResultStatus.TransportFailure,            
      162 +                    "The update succeeded but no new concurren
          +cy token could be obtained.");                                
      163 +            }                                                 
      164 +                                                              
      165 +            _logger.LogInformation("Updated {UserName}.", upda
          +te.UserName.Value);                                           
      166 +                                                              
      167 +            return Result<ConcurrencyToken>.Success(token);   
      168 +        }                                                     
      169 +        catch (Exception exception) when (IsRecoverable(except
          +ion, cancellationToken))                                      
      170 +        {                                                     
      171 +            return Recovered<ConcurrencyToken>(exception, requ
          +estUri);                                                      
      172 +        }                                                     
      173 +    }                                                         
      174 +                                                              
      175 +    private async Task<Result<PagedResult<PersonSummary>>> Que
          +ryAsync(                                                      
      176 +        PersonFilter filter,                                  
      177 +        int page,                                             
      178 +        int pageSize,                                         
      179 +        CancellationToken cancellationToken)                  
      180 +    {                                                         
      181 +        var builder = new ODataQueryBuilder()                 
      182 +            .Select(SummaryProperties)                        
      183 +            .Page(page, pageSize)                             
      184 +            .IncludeCount();                                  
      185 +                                                              
      186 +        // The filter only ever exists as a string from here i
          +nward.                                                        
      187 +        builder.Filter(_filterTranslator.Translate(filter));  
      188 +                                                              
      189 +        var requestUri = PeopleSet + builder.Build();         
      190 +                                                              
      191 +        try                                                   
      192 +        {                                                     
      193 +            _logger.LogDebug("GET {RequestUri}.", requestUri);
      194 +                                                              
      195 +            using var response = await _httpClient            
      196 +                .GetAsync(requestUri, cancellationToken)      
      197 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      198 +                                                              
      199 +            var status = _statusInterpreter.Interpret(response
          +.StatusCode, isSingleEntityRead: false);                      
      200 +                                                              
      201 +            if (status != ResultStatus.Success)               
      202 +            {                                                 
      203 +                return Failure<PagedResult<PersonSummary>>(sta
          +tus, response, requestUri);                                   
      204 +            }                                                 
      205 +                                                              
      206 +            var payload = await response.Content              
      207 +                .ReadFromJsonAsync<ODataCollectionResponse<Per
          +sonDto>>(JsonOptions, cancellationToken)                      
      208 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      209 +                                                              
      210 +            if (payload is null)                              
      211 +            {                                                 
      212 +                return Result<PagedResult<PersonSummary>>.Fail
          +ure(                                                          
      213 +                    ResultStatus.TransportFailure,            
      214 +                    "The service returned an empty collection 
          +response.");                                                  
      215 +            }                                                 
      216 +                                                              
      217 +            if (payload.NextLink is not null)                 
      218 +            {                                                 
      219 +                // Expected: the service pages at 8 rows whate
          +ver $top asks                                                 
      220 +                // for. Recorded so a short page is never mist
          +aken for the end                                              
      221 +                // of the data.                               
      222 +                _logger.LogDebug(                             
      223 +                    "The service truncated the page and suppli
          +ed a continuation link.");                                    
      224 +            }                                                 
      225 +                                                              
      226 +            var items = payload.Value.Select(_mapper.ToSummary
          +).ToArray();                                                  
      227 +                                                              
      228 +            _logger.LogDebug(                                 
      229 +                "Read {ItemCount} of {TotalCount} people.",   
      230 +                items.Length,                                 
      231 +                payload.Count ?? items.Length);               
      232 +                                                              
      233 +            return Result<PagedResult<PersonSummary>>.Success(
      234 +                new PagedResult<PersonSummary>(items, payload.
          +Count ?? items.Length, page, pageSize));                      
      235 +        }                                                     
      236 +        catch (Exception exception) when (IsRecoverable(except
          +ion, cancellationToken))                                      
      237 +        {                                                     
      238 +            return Recovered<PagedResult<PersonSummary>>(excep
          +tion, requestUri);                                            
      239 +        }                                                     
      240 +    }                                                         
      241 +                                                              
      242 +    private async Task<Result<Person>> ReadPersonAsync(       
      243 +        UserName userName,                                    
      244 +        bool forUpdate,                                       
      245 +        CancellationToken cancellationToken)                  
      246 +    {                                                         
      247 +        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(userName);          
      248 +                                                              
      249 +        var requestUri = PeopleSet + ODataQueryBuilder.KeySegm
          +ent(userName.Value);                                          
      250 +                                                              
      251 +        try                                                   
      252 +        {                                                     
      253 +            _logger.LogDebug(                                 
      254 +                "GET {RequestUri} ({ReadPurpose}).",          
      255 +                requestUri,                                   
      256 +                forUpdate ? "fresh read for update" : "read fo
          +r display");                                                  
      257 +                                                              
      258 +            using var response = await _httpClient            
      259 +                .GetAsync(requestUri, cancellationToken)      
      260 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      261 +                                                              
      262 +            var status = _statusInterpreter.Interpret(response
          +.StatusCode, isSingleEntityRead: true);                       
      263 +                                                              
      264 +            // The service answers 204 rather than 404 for a p
          +erson that does                                               
      265 +            // not exist, so this path is reached with an empt
          +y body and a                                                  
      266 +            // status a naive success check would accept.     
      267 +            if (status == ResultStatus.NotFound)              
      268 +            {                                                 
      269 +                _logger.LogDebug("No person named {UserName} e
          +xists.", userName.Value);                                     
      270 +                                                              
      271 +                return Result<Person>.NotFound($"No person nam
          +ed '{userName.Value}' exists.");                              
      272 +            }                                                 
      273 +                                                              
      274 +            if (status != ResultStatus.Success)               
      275 +            {                                                 
      276 +                return Failure<Person>(status, response, reque
          +stUri);                                                       
      277 +            }                                                 
      278 +                                                              
      279 +            var dto = await response.Content                  
      280 +                .ReadFromJsonAsync<PersonDto>(JsonOptions, can
          +cellationToken)                                               
      281 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      282 +                                                              
      283 +            if (dto is null)                                  
      284 +            {                                                 
      285 +                return Result<Person>.Failure(                
      286 +                    ResultStatus.TransportFailure,            
      287 +                    "The service returned an empty entity resp
          +onse.");                                                      
      288 +            }                                                 
      289 +                                                              
      290 +            dto.ETag ??= response.Headers.ETag?.ToString();   
      291 +                                                              
      292 +            return Result<Person>.Success(_mapper.ToDomain(dto
          +));                                                           
      293 +        }                                                     
      294 +        catch (Exception exception) when (IsRecoverable(except
          +ion, cancellationToken))                                      
      295 +        {                                                     
      296 +            return Recovered<Person>(exception, requestUri);  
      297 +        }                                                     
      298 +    }                                                         
      299 +                                                              
      300 +    /// <summary>                                             
      301 +    /// Takes the new token from the response header, falling 
          +back to a cheap                                               
      302 +    /// projected re-read if the service ever stops sending on
          +e.                                                            
      303 +    /// </summary>                                            
      304 +    private async Task<ConcurrencyToken?> ReadUpdatedTokenAsyn
          +c(                                                            
      305 +        UserName userName,                                    
      306 +        HttpResponseMessage response,                         
      307 +        CancellationToken cancellationToken)                  
      308 +    {                                                         
      309 +        var headerTag = response.Headers.ETag?.ToString();    
      310 +                                                              
      311 +        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(headerTag))            
      312 +        {                                                     
      313 +            return ConcurrencyToken.From(headerTag);          
      314 +        }                                                     
      315 +                                                              
      316 +        var requestUri = PeopleSet                            
      317 +            + ODataQueryBuilder.KeySegment(userName.Value)    
      318 +            + new ODataQueryBuilder().Select("UserName").Build
          +();                                                           
      319 +                                                              
      320 +        _logger.LogDebug("The update response carried no ETag;
          + re-reading {RequestUri}.", requestUri);                      
      321 +                                                              
      322 +        using var reread = await _httpClient                  
      323 +            .GetAsync(requestUri, cancellationToken)          
      324 +            .ConfigureAwait(false);                           
      325 +                                                              
      326 +        if (!reread.IsSuccessStatusCode)                      
      327 +        {                                                     
      328 +            return null;                                      
      329 +        }                                                     
      330 +                                                              
      331 +        var dto = await reread.Content                        
      332 +            .ReadFromJsonAsync<PersonDto>(JsonOptions, cancell
          +ationToken)                                                   
      333 +            .ConfigureAwait(false);                           
      334 +                                                              
      335 +        var tag = dto?.ETag ?? reread.Headers.ETag?.ToString()
          +;                                                             
      336 +                                                              
      337 +        return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tag) ? null : Concurr
          +encyToken.From(tag);                                          
      338 +    }                                                         
      339 +                                                              
      340 +    /// <summary>                                             
      341 +    /// Faults that belong in a result rather than propagating
          +. Cancellation                                                
      342 +    /// requested by the caller is excluded on purpose: that p
          +ropagates as                                                  
      343 +    /// <see cref="OperationCanceledException"/>, which is wha
          +t callers expect.                                             
      344 +    /// </summary>                                            
      345 +    private static bool IsRecoverable(Exception exception, Can
          +cellationToken cancellationToken) =>                          
      346 +        exception switch                                      
      347 +        {                                                     
      348 +            OperationCanceledException => !cancellationToken.I
          +sCancellationRequested,                                       
      349 +            HttpRequestException or JsonException or DomainExc
          +eption or InvalidOperationException =>                        
      350 +                true,                                         
      351 +            _ => false,                                       
      352 +        };                                                    
      353 +                                                              
      354 +    private Result<T> Recovered<T>(Exception exception, string
          + requestUri)                                                  
      355 +    {                                                         
      356 +        _logger.LogError(                                     
      357 +            exception,                                        
      358 +            "The OData request to {RequestUri} failed.",      
      359 +            requestUri);                                      
      360 +                                                              
      361 +        return Result<T>.Failure(ResultStatus.TransportFailure
          +, exception.Message);                                         
      362 +    }                                                         
      363 +                                                              
      364 +    private Result<T> Failure<T>(ResultStatus status, HttpResp
          +onseMessage response, string requestUri)                      
      365 +    {                                                         
      366 +        _logger.LogError(                                     
      367 +            "The OData request to {RequestUri} returned {Statu
          +sCode}.",                                                     
      368 +            requestUri,                                       
      369 +            (int)response.StatusCode);                        
      370 +                                                              
      371 +        return Result<T>.Failure(                             
      372 +            status,                                           
      373 +            $"The service returned {(int)response.StatusCode} 
          +for '{requestUri}'.");                                        
      374 +    }                                                         
      375  }

      107 +    private async Task<Result<T>> ReadThroughAsync<T>(        
      108 +        string cacheKey,                                      
      109 +        TimeSpan timeToLive,                                  
      110 +        bool isListEntry,                                     
      111 +        Func<Task<Result<T>>> read)                           
      112 +    {                                                         
      113 +        if (!_options.Enabled)                                
      114 +        {                                                     
      115 +            return await read().ConfigureAwait(false);        
      116 +        }                                                     
      117 +                                                              
      118 +        var pending = GetOrAdd(cacheKey, timeToLive, isListEnt
          +ry, read);                                                    
      119 +        var result = await pending.Value.ConfigureAwait(false)
          +;                                                             
      120 +                                                              
      121 +        // A failure must not occupy the entry for the whole T
          +TL, or one blip                                               
      122 +        // becomes a minute of cached errors.                 
      123 +        if (!result.IsSuccess)                                
      124 +        {                                                     
      125 +            _cache.Remove(cacheKey);                          
      126 +        }                                                     
      127 +                                                              
      128 +        return result;                                        
      129 +    }                                                         
      130 +                                                              
      131 +    /// <summary>                                             
      132 +    /// Double-checked insert of a <see cref="Lazy{T}"/> wrapp
          +ing the read.                                                 
      133 +    /// </summary>                                            
      134 +    /// <remarks>                                             
      135 +    /// The lock is held only while the Lazy is created and st
          +ored, never across                                            
      136 +    /// the await, so concurrent misses on one key share a sin
          +gle request                                                   
      137 +    /// without any caller blocking on another's I/O.         
      138 +    /// </remarks>                                            
      139 +    private Lazy<Task<Result<T>>> GetOrAdd<T>(                
      140 +        string cacheKey,                                      
      141 +        TimeSpan timeToLive,                                  
      142 +        bool isListEntry,                                     
      143 +        Func<Task<Result<T>>> read)                           
      144 +    {                                                         
      145 +        if (_cache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out Lazy<Task<Result<
          +T>>>? cached) && cached is not null)                          
      146 +        {                                                     
      147 +            _logger.LogDebug("Cache hit for {CacheKey}.", cach
          +eKey);                                                        
      148 +                                                              
      149 +            return cached;                                    
      150 +        }                                                     
      151 +                                                              
      152 +        lock (_gate)                                          
      153 +        {                                                     
      154 +            if (_cache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out cached) && ca
          +ched is not null)                                             
      155 +            {                                                 
      156 +                return cached;                                
      157 +            }                                                 
      158 +                                                              
      159 +            _logger.LogDebug("Cache miss for {CacheKey}.", cac
          +heKey);                                                       
      160 +                                                              
      161 +            var pending = new Lazy<Task<Result<T>>>(          
      162 +                read,                                         
      163 +                LazyThreadSafetyMode.ExecutionAndPublication);
      164 +                                                              
      165 +            var entryOptions = new MemoryCacheEntryOptions    
      166 +            {                                                 
      167 +                AbsoluteExpirationRelativeToNow = timeToLive, 
      168 +                Size = 1,                                     
      169 +            };                                                
      170 +                                                              
      171 +            if (isListEntry)                                  
      172 +            {                                                 
      173 +                entryOptions.AddExpirationToken(_keys.ListGene
          +ration);                                                      
      174 +            }                                                 
      175 +                                                              
      176 +            _cache.Set(cacheKey, pending, entryOptions);      
      177 +                                                              
      178 +            return pending;                                   
      179 +        }                                                     
      180 +    }                                                         
      181  }

       53 +        services.AddSingleton<ODataStatusInterpreter>();      
       54 +        services.AddSingleton<PeopleCacheKeys>();             
       55 +                                                              
       56 +        AddSessionResolution(services, settings);             
       57 +        AddODataClient(services, settings);                   
       58 +        AddCachingDecorator(services);                        
       59 +                                                              
       60          return services;
       61      }
       62 +                                                              
       63 +    /// <summary>                                             
       64 +    /// The resolver client is separate from the OData client 
          +and follows                                                   
       65 +    /// redirects, which is how the session address is discove
          +red at all.                                                   
       66 +    /// </summary>                                            
       67 +    private static void AddSessionResolution(IServiceCollectio
          +n services, TripPinOptions settings)                          
       68 +    {                                                         
       69 +        services.AddHttpClient(SessionUriProvider.ResolverClie
          +ntName, client =>                                             
       70 +            {                                                 
       71 +                client.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(settings
          +.RequestTimeoutSeconds);                                      
       72 +            })                                                
       73 +            .ConfigurePrimaryHttpMessageHandler(() => new Http
          +ClientHandler                                                 
       74 +            {                                                 
       75 +                AllowAutoRedirect = true,                     
       76 +            });                                               
       77 +                                                              
       78 +        // Singleton: a scoped provider would resolve a new se
          +ssion per scope,                                              
       79 +        // and every extra session starts from pristine data, 
          +silently                                                      
       80 +        // discarding earlier writes.                         
       81 +        services.AddSingleton<ISessionUriProvider, SessionUriP
          +rovider>();                                                   
       82 +        services.AddTransient<SessionUriHandler>();           
       83 +    }                                                         
       84 +                                                              
       85 +    private static void AddODataClient(IServiceCollection serv
          +ices, TripPinOptions settings)                                
       86 +    {                                                         
       87 +        services.AddHttpClient<ODataPeopleRepository>(client =
          +>                                                             
       88 +            {                                                 
       89 +                // The pre-redirect address. SessionUriHandler
          + swaps this prefix                                            
       90 +                // for the resolved session base on every requ
          +est.                                                          
       91 +                client.BaseAddress = EnsureTrailingSlash(setti
          +ngs.BaseAddress);                                             
       92 +                client.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(settings
          +.RequestTimeoutSeconds);                                      
       93 +                client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Accept.ParseAdd( 
       94 +                    "application/json;odata.metadata=minimal")
          +;                                                             
       95 +            })                                                
       96 +                                                              
       97 +            // Added first, so it is the outermost handler. A 
          +retry therefore                                               
       98 +            // re-enters SessionUriHandler and re-resolves the
          + session rather                                               
       99 +            // than replaying a request aimed at a dead one.  
      100 +            .AddTripPinResilience(settings.Resilience)        
      101 +            .AddHttpMessageHandler<SessionUriHandler>()       
      102 +            .ConfigurePrimaryHttpMessageHandler(() => new Http
          +ClientHandler                                                 
      103 +            {                                                 
      104 +                // Redirects are resolved once by the session 
          +provider; letting                                             
      105 +                // data requests follow them would mint sessio
          +ns unnoticed.                                                 
      106 +                AllowAutoRedirect = false,                    
      107 +            });                                               
      108 +    }                                                         
      109 +                                                              
      110 +    /// <summary>                                             
      111 +    /// Binds the port to the decorator, leaving both the use 
          +cases and                                                     
      112 +    /// ODataPeopleRepository unaware that caching exists.    
      113 +    /// </summary>                                            
      114 +    private static void AddCachingDecorator(IServiceCollection
          + services) =>                                                 
      115 +        services.AddScoped<IPeopleRepository>(provider => new 
          +CachedPeopleRepository(                                       
      116 +            provider.GetRequiredService<ODataPeopleRepository>
          +(),                                                           
      117 +            provider.GetRequiredService<IMemoryCache>(),      
      118 +            provider.GetRequiredService<PeopleCacheKeys>(),   
      119 +            provider.GetRequiredService<IOptions<TripPinOption
          +s>>(),                                                        
      120 +            provider.GetRequiredService<ILogger<CachedPeopleRe
          +pository>>()));                                               
      121 +                                                              
      122 +    private static Uri EnsureTrailingSlash(Uri uri) =>        
      123 +        uri.AbsoluteUri.EndsWith('/') ? uri : new Uri($"{uri.A
          +bsoluteUri}/");                                               
      124  }

       92 +    private async Task ResetDataSourceAsync()                 
       93 +    {                                                         
       94 +        try                                                   
       95 +        {                                                     
       96 +            var session = _provider!.GetRequiredService<ISessi
          +onUriProvider>();

          +c(CancellationToken.None)                                     
       98 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);

      100 +            var factory = _provider.GetRequiredService<IHttpCl
          +ientFactory>();

      102 +                                                              
      103 +            using var response = await client

          +ce"), null, CancellationToken.None)                           
      105 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      106 +        }

      16 +| Behaviour | Consequence for this codebase |                  
      17 +|---|---|                                                      
      18 +| Service root 302-redirects to a `(S(id))` session URL; each f
         +resh hit mints a new session with pristine data | `SessionUriPr
         +ovider` resolves once and shares it |

         + required on `PersonUpdate`, so a `428` is a defect, never retr

         +atusInterpreter` maps it to `NotFound` |

         +ple` collection** return `500` | `AddressInfo` is read-only and
         + never serialised (ADR-004) |

         +ed form, a `PATCH` body needs the bare name; each rejects the o
         +ther with `500` | `ODataFilterTranslator` and `PersonMapper` st
         +ay separate |

         +ion | The mapper emits `[]`, never null |

         +ordinal 0) | Domain `Gender` is non-nullable, with `Unknown` as
         + the third state |

         +ot an error | `PersonFilter` is structured; raw strings never c
         +ross the port |

         +d |

  Results

  dotnet build                                    0 Warning(s)  0 Error(s)

  dotnet test --filter "Category!=Integration"
    Domain.Tests            75 passed,   0 skipped

  Infrastructure went from 1 passing / 18 skipped to 114 passing / 0 skipped,
  filling the four existing folders: QueryBuilding (36), QuirkHandling (52),
  Caching (26).

  The one thing you should know about

  Integration testing found a new service quirk that the unit tests could not 
  have caught, and it was a real bug in my first implementation.

  @odata.count is computed after $top/$skip, which the OData spec forbids.
  Measured:

  $count=true              -> 20   (the true total)
  $count=true&$top=8       ->  8   (just the page length)
  $count=true&$top=4       ->  4
  $count=true&$skip=16     ->  4

  ListAsync originally read the count from the paged response, so TotalCount
  came back as 8 instead of 20. That is a silent data-hiding bug: HasMore goes
  false, and the console would show "page 1 of 1" for every query while quietly
  concealing twelve people. No exception, no error status.

  Fix: QueryAsync now issues two requests, one for the total with no $top/$skip
  and one for the page with no $count. The extra round trip is absorbed by the
  caching decorator on repeat views. Two stub tests now guard it, including
  The_total_is_never_taken_from_the_paged_response, which fails if anyone
  re-collapses them.

  This is exactly what integration tests are for, and it is the strongest
  argument for keeping that folder.

  Notable implementation points

  The corruption regression exists in two forms. The stubbed version does not
  just assert on the payload; the stub models the poisoning, flipping a flag if
  AddressInfo ever appears and then returning 500 from all subsequent reads.
  The_corruption_model_does_fail_when_AddressInfo_is_sent proves that stub would
  actually catch a regression rather than passing vacuously. The live version
  performs a real sparse PATCH and asserts both the detail read and the full
  collection read still succeed.

  Session redirect following is verified live, not stubbed. Redirect following
  belongs to the primary handler, so MockHttpMessageHandler cannot emulate it.
  What is unit-tested is everything else: 32 concurrent callers producing
  exactly one resolution, Invalidate forcing re-resolution, a failed resolution
  not being cached permanently, and Rebase preserving quoted key segments and
  query strings. The redirect itself is asserted in
  The_service_root_redirects_to_a_session_scoped_address.

  Stampede protection is measured, not assumed. 32 concurrent misses on one key
  produce one inner call, and every caller receives the same instance, not
  merely an equal one.

  Integration teardown is doubly contained. xUnit builds a fresh instance per
  test, so each test resolves its own session and therefore its own pristine
  data; and DisposeAsync calls ResetDataSource whether the test passed or
  failed. Teardown swallows its own transport errors so it can never convert a
  pass into a fail or mask a real assertion failure.

  Two small decisions

  I added <InternalsVisibleTo Include="TripPin.Infrastructure.Tests" /> so
  SessionUriHandler.Rebase could be tested directly without widening the public
  surface. And I relaxed two analyzer rules to suggestion with reasons in
  .editorconfig: CA1822 (wrong for DI-injected collaborators, where making
  methods static defeats the injection) and CA1873 (every logging call here sits
  next to a network round trip; the one genuinely allocating argument, a
  string.Join, is hand-guarded with IsEnabled).

  I also appended a measured service quirks table to docs/adr/README.md,
  covering all twelve behaviours with the consequence each has for the codebase.
  That is where the count quirk is now recorded.

  The Console layer is still placeholders, so Program.cs builds the host and
  exits without running the menu loop. That is the remaining work.

✻ Sautéed for 26m 34s

> Implement Console per the approved design (Menus/, Rendering/, Input/ —       
  PersonRenderer already exists, extend it as needed). Use                      
  Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting generic host with DI, wired against              
  IPeopleRepository (via CachedPeopleRepository from the container, Console     
  never sees caching or HTTP directly).                                         
                                                                                
  Menus:                                                                        
  - List people: paged via ListAsync/PagedResult, using the real TotalCount     
  from Infrastructure show "page X of Y", handle HasMore correctly given the    
  count-quirk fix.                                                              
  - Search/filter: build a PersonFilter from prompted fields (confirm against   
  the metadata which are sensible to filter on likely name and email), call     
  SearchAsync.                                                                  
  - View details: GetByIdAsync (cached path is fine here), render via           
  PersonRenderer including the read-only AddressInfo list (no edit affordance   
  for it, per ADR-004).                                                         
  - Update: call GetForUpdateAsync (never the cached GetByIdAsync) immediately  
  before editing, so the ConcurrencyToken is fresh. Prompt only for fields      
  being changed untouched fields must stay null in the resulting PersonUpdate,  
  not be re-sent. For Emails specifically, make clearing a distinct explicit    
  action from leaving unchanged (null vs [] surface this as two different menu  
  options or a clear prompt, not one ambiguous "edit emails" that can't         
  distinguish the two). Send PersonUpdate with the fresh token via UpdateAsync. 
  - Confirm-before-write: show a summary of the changed fields only, require    
  explicit confirmation before calling UpdateAsync.                             
  - On Result.Conflict (stale token / 412): tell the user the record changed    
  since they loaded it and offer to reload and retry, don't silently retry and  
  don't just fail.                                                              
                                                                                
  Cancellation:                                                                 
  - Every call takes a CancellationToken composed via OperationScope:           
  Console.CancelKeyPress (e.Cancel = true for graceful shutdown) + a            
  per-operation timeout + last-one-wins supersede for overlapping searches, per 
  the design already agreed.                                                    
                                                                                
  Error handling:                                                               
  - Map Result<T> failure cases (NotFound, ValidationFailed, Conflict) to       
  plain-language console messages. Any unexpected exception is logged via       
  ILogger (full detail) and shown to the user as a generic "something went      
  wrong, see logs" never a raw stack trace or exception message on screen.      
                                                                                
  Config:                                                                       
  - Read from appsettings.json (BaseUrl, timeouts, cache TTLs, retry/breaker    
  knobs) already scaffolded in TripPinOptions.                                  
                                                                                
  No Console.Tests project, per our earlier decision if OperationScope's        
  cancel/supersede logic needs deeper verification, that's already covered in a 
  lower layer or flag it back to me rather than standing up a test project      
  here.                                                                         
                                                                                
  Keep this layer thin orchestration, prompting, formatting only, no business   
  rules. Show me the code, then run it against the live service so I can see    
  the full menu flow (list → search → detail → update) actually work end to     
  end.

      35 +    public CancellationToken Begin(TimeSpan timeout)           
      36 +    {                                                          
      37 +        var next = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSou
         +rce(_applicationStopping);                                     
      38 +        next.CancelAfter(timeout);                             
      39  
      40 +        Supersede(Interlocked.Exchange(ref _current, next));   
      41 +                                                               
      42 +        return next.Token;                                     
      43 +    }                                                          
      44 +                                                               
      45      /// <summary>Cancels the in-flight operation, if any, witho
          ut starting another.</summary>
      31 -    public void CancelCurrent() => throw new NotImplementedExce
         -ption();                                                       
      46 +    public void CancelCurrent() => Supersede(Interlocked.Exchan
         +ge(ref _current, null));                                       
      47  
      33 -    public void Dispose() => throw new NotImplementedException(
         -);                                                             
      48 +    public void Dispose() => CancelCurrent();                  
      49 +                                                               
      50 +    /// <summary>                                              
      51 +    /// Cancels then releases a superseded source.             
      52 +    /// </summary>                                             
      53 +    /// <remarks>                                              
      54 +    /// Disposal immediately after cancelling is safe here beca
         +use the menu loop                                              
      55 +    /// awaits each action to completion, so nothing is still h
         +olding the token                                               
      56 +    /// by the time the next action begins. Supersession is def
         +ensive, for the                                                
      57 +    /// day an overlapping request path is added.              
      58 +    /// </remarks>                                             
      59 +    private static void Supersede(CancellationTokenSource? prev
         +ious)                                                          
      60 +    {                                                          
      61 +        if (previous is null)                                  
      62 +        {                                                      
      63 +            return;                                            
      64 +        }                                                      
      65 +                                                               
      66 +        try                                                    
      67 +        {                                                      
      68 +            previous.Cancel();                                 
      69 +            previous.Dispose();                                
      70 +        }                                                      
      71 +        catch (ObjectDisposedException)                        
      72 +        {                                                      
      73 +            // Already released by a concurrent supersede.     
      74 +        }                                                      
      75 +    }                                                          
      76  }

       46 +        OperationScope scope,                                 
       47 +        TimeSpan operationTimeout,                            
       48 +        CancellationToken inputToken)                         
       49 +    {                                                         
       50 +        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(userName);          
       51 +        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(scope);             
       52 +                                                              
       53 +        while (!inputToken.IsCancellationRequested)           
       54 +        {                                                     
       55 +            // Always the uncached read. A cached entity carri
          +es a cached ETag,                                             
       56 +            // and a stale ETag is a rejected write.          
       57 +            var loaded = await _repository                    
       58 +                .GetForUpdateAsync(userName, scope.Begin(opera
          +tionTimeout))                                                 
       59 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
       60 +                                                              
       61 +            if (!loaded.IsSuccess)                            
       62 +            {                                                 
       63 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine();                    
       64 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine($"  {_presenter.Descri
          +be(loaded)}");                                                
       65 +                return;                                       
       66 +            }                                                 
       67 +                                                              
       68 +            var person = loaded.Value!;                       
       69 +                                                              
       70 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine();                        
       71 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine($"--- Edit {person.UserNam
          +e.Value} ---");                                               
       72 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine(_renderer.RenderDetail(per
          +son));                                                        
       73 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine();                        
       74 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine("  Leave a field blank to 
          +leave it unchanged.");                                        
       75 +                                                              
       76 +            var command = await CollectAsync(person, inputToke
          +n).ConfigureAwait(false);                                     
       77 +            var changes = Summarize(person, command);         
       78 +                                                              
       79 +            if (changes.Count == 0)                           
       80 +            {                                                 
       81 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine();                    
       82 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine("  Nothing was changed
          +, so nothing was saved.");                                    
       83 +                return;                                       
       84 +            }                                                 
       85 +                                                              
       86 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine();                        
       87 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine("  --- Changes to save ---
          +");                                                           
       88 +                                                              
       89 +            foreach (var change in changes)                   
       90 +            {                                                 
       91 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine($"    {change}");     
       92 +            }                                                 
       93 +                                                              
       94 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine();                        
       95 +                                                              
       96 +            if (!await _reader.ConfirmAsync("  Save these chan
          +ges?", inputToken).ConfigureAwait(false))                     
       97 +            {                                                 
       98 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine("  Nothing was saved."
          +);                                                            
       99 +                return;                                       
      100 +            }                                                 
      101 +                                                              
      102 +            var result = await _handler                       
      103 +                .HandleAsync(command, scope.Begin(operationTim
          +eout))                                                        
      104 +                .ConfigureAwait(false);                       
      105 +                                                              
      106 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine();                        
      107 +                                                              
      108 +            if (result.IsSuccess)                             
      109 +            {                                                 
      110 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine($"  Saved. New version
          +: {result.Value!.Value}");                                    
      111 +                return;                                       
      112 +            }                                                 
      113 +                                                              
      114 +            // A conflict is the expected outcome of two peopl
          +e editing at once.                                            
      115 +            // Never retried silently: the record on the serve
          +r is not what this                                            
      116 +            // user was looking at, so they have to see the ne
          +w state first.                                                
      117 +            if (result.Status == ResultStatus.ConcurrencyConfl
          +ict)                                                          
      118 +            {                                                 
      119 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine($"  {_presenter.Descri
          +be(result)}");                                                
      120 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine("  Someone else change
          +d this person while you were editing.");                      
      121 +                                                              
      122 +                if (await _reader                             
      123 +                    .ConfirmAsync("  Reload the current values
          + and try again?", inputToken)                                 
      124 +                    .ConfigureAwait(false))                   
      125 +                {                                             
      126 +                    continue;                                 
      127 +                }                                             
      128 +                                                              
      129 +                SystemConsole.WriteLine("  Nothing was saved."
          +);                                                            
      130 +                return;                                       
      131 +            }                                                 
      132 +                                                              
      133 +            SystemConsole.WriteLine($"  {_presenter.Describe(r
          +esult)}");                                                    
      134 +            return;                                           
      135 +        }                                                     
      136 +    }                                                         
      137 +                                                              
      138 +    /// <summary>                                             
      139 +    /// Builds the command. Untouched fields stay null so they
          + are never sent.                                              
      140 +    /// </summary>                                            
      141 +    private async Task<UpdatePersonCommand> CollectAsync(Perso
          +n person, CancellationToken inputToken)                       
      142 +    {                                                         
      143 +        var firstName = await _reader                         
      144 +            .PromptOptionalAsync("  First name", person.Name.F
          +irst, inputToken)                                             
      145 +            .ConfigureAwait(false);                           
      146 +                                                              
      147 +        var lastName = await _reader                          
      148 +            .PromptOptionalAsync("  Last name ", person.Name.L
          +ast, inputToken)                                              
      149 +            .ConfigureAwait(false);                           
      150 +                                                              
      151 +        var gender = await PromptGenderAsync(person, inputToke
          +n).ConfigureAwait(false);                                     
      152 +        var emails = await PromptEmailsAsync(inputToken).Confi
          +gureAwait(false);                                             
      153 +                                                              
      154 +        return new UpdatePersonCommand                        
      155 +        {                                                     
      156 +            UserName = person.UserName,                       
      157 +                                                              
      158 +            // The token from the read above, not a re-fetched
          + one. This is what                                            
      159 +            // makes a conflict mean "somebody else changed it
          +".                                                            
      160 +            Concurrency = person.Concurrency,                 
      161 +                                                              
      162 +            FirstName = Blank(firstName) ? null : firstName!.T
          +rim(),                                                        
      163 +            LastName = Blank(lastName) ? null : lastName!.Trim
          +(),                                                           
      164 +            Gender = gender,                                  
      165 +            Emails = emails,                                  
      166 +        };                                                    
      167 +    }                                                         
      168 +                                                              
      169 +    private async Task<Gender?> PromptGenderAsync(Person perso
          +n, CancellationToken inputToken)                              
      170 +    {                                                         
      171 +        var answer = await _reader                            
      172 +            .PromptOptionalAsync(                             
      173 +                "  Gender (male/female/unknown)",             
      174 +                person.Gender.ToString(),                     
      175 +                inputToken)                                   
      176 +            .ConfigureAwait(false);                           
      177 +                                                              
      178 +        if (Blank(answer))                                    
      179 +        {                                                     
      180 +            return null;                                      
      181 +        }                                                     
      182 +                                                              
      183 +        if (Enum.TryParse<Gender>(answer!.Trim(), ignoreCase: 
          +true, out var gender)                                         
      184 +            && Enum.IsDefined(gender))                        
      185 +        {                                                     
      186 +            return gender;                                    
      187 +        }                                                     
      188 +                                                              
      189 +        SystemConsole.WriteLine("    Unrecognised gender; leav
          +ing it unchanged.");                                          
      190 +        return null;                                          
      191 +    }                                                         
      192 +                                                              
      193 +    /// <summary>                                             
      194 +    /// Three explicit outcomes, because the wire distinguishe
          +s them.                                                       
      195 +    /// </summary>                                            
      196 +    /// <remarks>                                             
      197 +    /// Keeping and clearing cannot share a prompt: null leave
          +s the collection                                              
      198 +    /// alone while an empty list wipes it, and the service re
          +jects an explicit                                             
      199 +    /// null with a 500. A single "edit emails" prompt where b
          +lank meant one or                                             
      200 +    /// the other would make the destructive option the easies
          +t to hit by                                                   
      201 +    /// accident.                                             
      202 +    /// </remarks>                                            
      203 +    private async Task<IReadOnlyList<string>?> PromptEmailsAsy
          +nc(CancellationToken inputToken)                              
      204 +    {                                                         
      205 +        SystemConsole.WriteLine("  Emails:");                 
      206 +        SystemConsole.WriteLine("    [k] keep as they are    [
          +r] replace with a new list    [c] clear all");                
      207 +                                                              
      208 +        var choice = await _reader.PromptAsync("    > ", input
          +Token).ConfigureAwait(false);                                 
      209 +                                                              
      210 +        switch (choice?.Trim().ToLowerInvariant())            
      211 +        {                                                     
      212 +            case "c":                                         
      213 +                return [];                                    
      214 +                                                              
      215 +            case "r":                                         
      216 +                var entered = await _reader                   
      217 +                    .PromptAsync("    New emails (comma separa
          +ted): ", inputToken)                                          
      218 +                    .ConfigureAwait(false);                   
      219 +                                                              
      220 +                if (Blank(entered))                           
      221 +                {                                             
      222 +                    // Refused rather than guessed: an empty a
          +nswer here could                                              
      223 +                    // mean either intent, and one of them des
          +troys data.                                                   
      224 +                    SystemConsole.WriteLine(                  
      225 +                        "    Nothing entered. Use [c] to clear
          + them. Leaving them unchanged.");                             
      226 +                                                              
      227 +                    return null;                              
      228 +                }                                             
      229 +                                                              
      230 +                return entered!                               
      231 +                    .Split(',', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries
          + | StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);                    
      232 +                                                              
      233 +            default:                                          
      234 +                return null;                                  
      235 +        }                                                     
      236 +    }                                                         
      237 +                                                              
      238 +    /// <summary>Describes only the fields that will actually 
          +be sent.</summary>                                            
      239 +    private static List<string> Summarize(Person person, Updat
          +ePersonCommand command)                                       
      240 +    {                                                         
      241 +        var changes = new List<string>(4);                    
      242 +                                                              
      243 +        if (command.FirstName is not null && command.FirstName
          + != person.Name.First)                                        
      244 +        {                                                     
      245 +            changes.Add($"First name : {person.Name.First} -> 
          +{command.FirstName}");                                        
      246 +        }                                                     
      247 +                                                              
      248 +        if (command.LastName is not null && command.LastName !
          += person.Name.Last)                                           
      249 +        {                                                     
      250 +            changes.Add($"Last name  : {person.Name.Last} -> {
          +command.LastName}");                                          
      251 +        }                                                     
      252 +                                                              
      253 +        if (command.Gender is not null && command.Gender != pe
          +rson.Gender)                                                  
      254 +        {                                                     
      255 +            changes.Add($"Gender     : {person.Gender} -> {com
          +mand.Gender}");                                               
      256 +        }                                                     
      257 +                                                              
      258 +        if (command.Emails is not null)                       
      259 +        {                                                     
      260 +            changes.Add(command.Emails.Count == 0             
      261 +                ? "Emails     : cleared (all removed)"        
      262 +                : $"Emails     : replaced with {string.Join(",
          + ", command.Emails)}");                                       
      263 +        }                                                     
      264 +                                                              
      265 +        return changes;                                       
      266 +    }                                                         
      267 +                                                              
      268 +    private static bool Blank(string? value) => string.IsNullO
          +rWhiteSpace(value);                                           
      269  }

  gets sent.

      183 +    private bool TryParse(string? entered, out UserName userNa
          +me)                                                           
      184 +    {

          +error))

  Verification

  dotnet build                                    0 Warning(s)  0 Error(s)

  The four flows, run live

  List — the count fix is visible in the footer:
    Page 1 of 3  (20 people)
    [n]ext  [v]iew a person  [b]ack
  Navigating back to page 1 issued no HTTP request, so the cache decorator hit.

  Search — all three PersonFilter fields confirmed against the metadata and live
  service (name, email, gender). russ + female correctly matched nobody, since
  Russell is Male; the contoso email filter found 16.

  Detail — AddressInfo renders with an explicit (read-only) marker and no edit
  affordance anywhere, per ADR-004.

  Update — the summary shows only what will actually be sent:
    --- Changes to save ---
      First name : Russell -> Russell-Updated
    Save these changes? (y/N): y
    Saved. New version: W/"08DEEF1CDE38C94D"
  The subsequent view showed both the new value and the new version, proving
  cache invalidation.

  Conflict handling, proven with a real 412

  I drove a genuine conflict by having a competing writer modify the record
  inside the console's own session while the console sat at its first prompt
  holding an already-read token:

    Version   : W/"08DEEF1DB07B5989"        <- console's token
    ### competing PATCH -> HTTP 204          <- rival changes LastName

    Save these changes? (y/N): y
    This person changed since you loaded them, so nothing was saved.
    Someone else changed this person while you were editing.
    Reload the current values and try again? (y/N): y

    Name      : Russell ChangedByRival       <- rival's change now visible
    Version   : W/"08DEEF1DB233E927"         <- fresh token
    Saved. New version: W/"08DEEF1DB9EF0A7B"

  Final state was ConflictWon ChangedByRival: no lost update. The rival's change
  survived and the user's applied on top. Nothing was retried silently, and
  nothing just failed.

  Emails: null vs empty, as two distinct actions

  The prompt is [k] keep as they are  [r] replace with a new list  [c] clear 
  all, never one ambiguous "edit emails". Live results: [c] produced Emails : 
  cleared (all removed) and the re-read showed (none). [r] with blank input
  refused to guess rather than defaulting to the destructive reading:

      Nothing entered. Use [c] to clear them. Leaving them unchanged.
    Nothing was changed, so nothing was saved.

  Two bugs the live run caught

  A raw stack trace reached the screen. builder.Build() sat outside my try
  block, so a bad config line crashed with ~60 lines of Serilog and DI internals
  — precisely what you said must never happen. Startup is now guarded
  separately, with the detail going to stderr and only the generic sentence to
  stdout.

  JSON has no comments. I put a "//" explanatory key in the Serilog Override
  section; Serilog read it as a logger category name and threw. Removed. (That
  was the config error that surfaced the bug above, so it paid for itself.)

  I also set "Polly": "Warning" — Polly logs every successful attempt at
  Information, which drowned the menu. Our own OnRetry/OnOpened handlers still
  log retries and circuit transitions regardless.

  One design note worth flagging

  PersonEditScreen takes IPeopleRepository alongside the command handler, purely
  for the pre-edit GetForUpdateAsync. That is an Application-owned port, so the
  dependency direction holds, but it is the one place Console touches a port
  rather than a use case. The alternative would be a GetPersonForEdit query
  handler that does nothing but delegate. I judged the extra indirection not
  worth it for four use cases; say the word if you'd rather have the symmetry.

  OperationScope stayed simple enough not to need its own tests, per our
  agreement: it links three tokens and swaps one via Interlocked.Exchange. If an
  overlapping-request path is ever added, that is the trigger to extract and
  test it.

● Coverage review done. The audit found three real defects, not just missing
  tests.

  Results

  dotnet build                                    0 Warning(s)  0 Error(s)

  dotnet test --filter "Category!=Integration"
    Domain.Tests             75 passed  (unchanged)
    Application.Tests       110 passed  (was 74, +36)
    Infrastructure.Tests    165 passed  (was 114, +51)
                          ──────────────────────────
                            350 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped

  dotnet test --filter "Category=Integration"
                             15 passed, 0 failed  (39s, live endpoint)

  Defects found

  1. A 400 reported the service as unreachable. ODataStatusInterpreter mapped
  everything unrecognised to TransportFailure, so a malformed request produced
  "The TripPin service could not be reached. Please try again shortly." That
  sends whoever investigates to the network instead of the query. Now maps to
  ValidationFailed, and the repository parses the OData error envelope so the
  service's own message surfaces ("The query option '$filter' can only be 
  applied to collection resouces") instead of a generic string. The innererror
  stack trace this service also sends is deliberately not surfaced.

  2. A genuine timeout was never retried. This is the one your question directly
  uncovered. My predicate matched HttpRequestException or TimeoutException, but
  an HttpClient timeout arrives as TaskCanceledException wrapping a
  TimeoutException — neither branch matched, so the most common transient fault 
  of all silently skipped the retry pipeline. The fix has to distinguish a
  timeout from a caller abandoning the request, since both are
  OperationCanceledException; the predicate now consults the caller's own token
  to tell them apart.

  3. MaxRetryAttempts: 0 crashed at startup. [Range(0, 10)] permits zero, but
  Polly rejects it with a ValidationException during pipeline construction. Zero
  now means "retries disabled" rather than a startup crash.

  The resilience pipeline had zero tests — every other Infrastructure test wires
  mockHttp.ToHttpClient() directly, bypassing Polly entirely. That's why
  defects 2 and 3 survived.

  What was added

  Application (+36) — UpdatePersonValidatorEdgeCaseTests: exotic whitespace
  (tab, CRLF, non-breaking space) for names and emails; all eleven malformed
  email shapes asserted through the validator rather than only in Domain; index
  correctness on a mixed list; length limits propagating; gender boundary
  values.

  On your "every optional field" point — the answer is an asymmetry worth
  stating outright, so Emails_is_the_only_field_with_a_clear_operation asserts
  it directly. Names are required, so blank is a validation error, never an
  erasure. Gender has no representable empty state (a null write coerces to
  Male), so Unknown carries that meaning. Only the email collection genuinely
  distinguishes null from empty.

  Infrastructure (+51) — ResiliencePipelineTests (22) builds a real
  Polly-wrapped client via AddTripPinResilience: transient statuses retried,
  timeouts retried, 412/428/400 attempted exactly once, caller-cancellation not
  retried, and conflicts never opening the circuit. ErrorPayloadTests (18)
  covers the 400 mapping and malformed error bodies. CacheInvalidationValueTests
  (11) closes the two gaps you named.

  On the cache tests specifically

  You were right that the existing invalidation tests were thin — they asserted
  GetByIdCalls == 2, which proves a call happened but not what the caller
  received. The new ones assert the value, with two control tests
  (Without_an_update_a_cached_detail_stays_stale) proving the harness can
  actually detect staleness rather than passing vacuously.

  For concurrent different-filter searches, each result carries its own filter
  text, so a crossover is directly observable. 24 concurrent distinct filters
  produce 24 distinct entries, every caller gets its own filter's result, and a
  second sequential pass is served entirely from cache — proving no entry
  displaced another.

  One behaviour worth flagging as intentional:
  A_different_persons_detail_entry_survives_an_unrelated_update documents that
  editing person A leaves person B's detail entry cached (only A's is evicted by
  key), while all list and search entries refresh. That is the design from
  ADR-002, but it was previously unstated, so it is now pinned by a test rather
  than left as an assumption.

✻ Cooked for 11m 26s
